- **metabolite-gene-functional-linkage**: Use when metabolite statistics data frames with KEGG compound identifiers (e.g. metabolites with adjusted p values &lt;= 0.05) need functional orthology and gene metadata for hypothesis generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-gene-functional-linkage))
- **metabolite-gene-identifier-mapping**: Use when a user supplies a list of metabolite and/or gene identifiers of mixed origin (hmdb, kegg, ensembl, uniprot, CAS, pubchem, chebi...) that must be translated to RaMP pathway IDs before Fisher enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-gene-identifier-mapping))
- **metabolite-gene-interaction-mapping**: Use when you have a standalone list of disease-associated metabolites (e.g. 39 plasma lipids or 56 urine metabolites) and need to convert them into gene sets for over-representation analysis against GWAS subnetworks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-gene-interaction-mapping))
- **metabolite-gene-intersection-analysis**: Use when you have an intersection gene set from a metabolic-vs-genomic hypergeometric comparison and need to determine, gene by gene, whether each gene sits at a primary keyword node or a first-neighbour node in the GWAS subnetwork. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-gene-intersection-analysis))
- **metabolite-gene-network-enrichment-visualisation**: Use when you have cluster-level differentially expressed metabolites and/or genes from an integrated spatial multi-omics object and need regional pathway enrichment plus an Enrichment Map network linking pathway feature sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-gene-network-enrichment-visualisation))
- **metabolite-generation-logic-mapping**: Use when when you have access to the MAGMa source code and need to understand or audit how in silico metabolite candidates are enumerated from parent structures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/metabolite-generation-logic-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-genomic-network-mapping**: Use when you have independent metabolomic GWAS results (metabolite p-values, effect sizes) and separate meta-genome GWAS results (variant p-values, effect sizes) from similar diseases or phenotypes, and you want to identify statistically enriched metabolite–gene co-occurrences without paired. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-genomic-network-mapping))
- **metabolite-hadamard-embedding-layer-design**: Use when building or reimplementing a transformer model whose inputs are tabular metabolite (or similar numeric feature) vectors and you need feature-to-token embeddings plus interaction-aware attention biasing for pre-training or fine-tuning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-hadamard-embedding-layer-design))
- **metabolite-hit-table-compilation**: Use when you have searched query spectra (e.g., CSA_MSP .msp composite spectra) against an MS/MS library (e.g., NIST 2020) with IDSL.FSA and need to extract and tabulate each spectrum's -scoring annotation into a CSV hit table. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-hit-table-compilation))
- **metabolite-hub-identification**: Use when a metabolomics differential analysis has produced significant metabolites (|fold change| ≥ 1, adjusted p &lt; 0.05) and you need to rank them as network hubs by relative betweenness centrality on a KEGG pathway–metabolite network. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-hub-identification))
- **metabolite-id-normalization**: Use when you have metabolite identifiers sourced from or annotated against heterogeneous metabolome databases (HMDB, ChEBI, PubChem, KEGG, LipidMaps) and need to resolve ambiguity in metabolite identity, create a unified metabolite reference table, or enable cross-database queries in a metabolomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-id-normalization))
- **metabolite-identification-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when comparing two MS/MS spectral matching strategies (e.g., longitudinal fragment profiles vs cross-sectional entropy) on annotation accuracy and FDR per mode, using ROC-derived thresholds and a labeled cohort like CKD plasma. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identification-accuracy-benchmarking))
- **metabolite-identification-annotation**: Use when MS/MS spectra (MSP/MGF) remain unmatched to spectral libraries (e.g. ARUS spectra) and candidate compound annotations are needed, with formulas assigned (e.g. BUDDY) and a precursor m/z available for filtering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identification-annotation))
- **metabolite-identification-benchmarking**: Use when evaluating a spectral-to-structure ranking model on labeled test spectra (MSP) against a fixed reference compound library, to reproduce or compare top-k annotation accuracy metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identification-benchmarking))
- **metabolite-identification-from-transformation-rules**: Use when when you have a small-molecule structure (SMILES, MOL, or SDF format) and need to identify probable metabolites or degradation products in a specific biological compartment (e.g., soil/aquatic microbiota, mammalian liver, or gut microbiota). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-identification-from-transformation-rules) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-identification-model-fitting**: Use when merged GCxGC/TOF-MS replicate peak lists must be scored against a reference metabolite library to identify which library metabolites are present, with posterior confidence per assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identification-model-fitting))
- **metabolite-identification-result-tabulation**: Use when DeepMASS structural-similarity scoring has produced ranked candidate metabolites for MS/MS spectra (e.g. 662 KEGG compounds or male-infertility biomarkers) and you need per-compound result tables plus aggregate validity counts. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identification-result-tabulation))
- **metabolite-identification-validation**: Use when you have extended a metabolite identification tool (such as Met-ID) to support a new derivatizing matrix beyond the default (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-identification-validation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-identifier-annotation**: Use when you have observed compounds (from LC-MS/MS, GC-MS, NMR, or other analytical techniques) with unknown identity and you want to assign candidate metabolite structures by comparing them to computationally predicted metabolism pathways. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-identifier-annotation) · 4 copies)
- **metabolite-identifier-conversion**: Use when your metabolomics dataset contains metabolite identifiers in multiple formats (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-identifier-conversion) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-identifier-cross-mapping**: Use when when you have metabolite identifiers from one or more metabolome databases and need to resolve them to equivalent identifiers in other databases, or when standardizing metabolite representation across multi-source metabolomic datasets where compounds may be annotated using different. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-identifier-cross-mapping))
- **metabolite-identifier-harmonization-across-reconstructions**: Use when an external biomass objective function (e.g. the VBOF from Renz et al., 2020) must be added to a generic reconstruction such as Recon2.04 or Recon3D_01 whose metabolite identifiers use a different namespace, before running FASTCC and exporting consistent infected/control SBML models. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-identifier-harmonization-across-reconstructions))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when metabolite features are named with retention-time codes (e.g. RI1150.81) or other non-standard identifiers and they must be converted to standard database IDs for KEGG pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--fae1b6c2b2e4e072))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when you have a table of InChIKeys from merged metabolomics datasets and need to attach MW, formula, and cross-database IDs (ChEBI, HMDB, KEGG, LipidMaps, DrugBank, CAS) for interstudy comparability. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--45fcf87f21d960b0))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when you have a list of top-ranked compound names and formulas from MS/MS structure annotation (e.g. NIST hybrid search or CSI:FingerID output) and need standardized InChI keys and chemical-ontology classes for the annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--65e499d246a58229))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when a metabolomics-to-genomics pipeline needs to convert KEGG pathway metabolites into HMDB IDs and then into UniProt proteins/HGNC gene symbols, e.g. to build a metabolite-interacting gene set for over-representation analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--a397fcc261b64fdb))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when a metabolomics input table contains sparse primary database identifiers (e.g. HMDB, ChEBI, KEGG, PubChem, LIPID MAPS) with empty foreign-ID fields that must be filled for pathway enrichment or cross-database linkage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--ff26d863403ae25b))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when a study needs ROIAL-NMR to run against a metabolite NMR reference other than the HMDB — e.g., building a BMRB-derived library from metabolite 1H chemical shift assignments for user-selected database loading. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--2053a951f701fed1))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when combining metabolomics studies that report the same metabolite under different names/synonyms, and compound-level aggregation (e.g. vote-counting in amanida) would otherwise split or mismatch hits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--4ea9e169300b5052))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when the input is a metabolomics feature matrix whose columns (or rows) are raw metabolite names (e.g. 227 plasma or 162 tissue metabolites) and you need standard HMDB IDs plus pathway assignments before computing pathway deregulation scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--ba79361fd86ea38f))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when building a literature-derived metabolite catalog from supplementary tables and mapping each entry against database indexation (e.g., MetaCyc) and GSMN producibility to quantify indexed vs. orphan coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--dc35a0cefd1a333b))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when a list of differential metabolites from LC-MS/MS untargeted or targeted metabolomics (e.g., from XCMS processing of &gt;20,000 m/z values) needs functional pathway annotation, and you must first reconcile compound identifiers against HMDB before submitting to KEGG-based enrichment tools. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--2fb21979a9995b2b))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when annotating preprocessed MSI feature lists (measured m/z values) with metabolite names and adduct forms using an organ-specific metabolite database, e.g. mapping brain/liver/kidney/heart/spleen/lung/muscle/pancreas/serum metabolites from AFADESI-MSI mouse body data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--ad43655178096db3))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when working with scFEA model files such as cmMat_171.csv, module_gene_m168.csv, or cName_c70_m168.csv and compound IDs must be joined to names or classified as intermediate substrates connecting modules. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping--233b00464da7ac5e))
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping**: Use when after peak detection and MS1 feature picking from merged FIA-MS spectra (typically 0–30 s acquisition window), when you have a list of accurate monoisotopic masses and need to assign HMDB compound identifiers, molecular formulas, and structural annotations to support mzTab output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-identifier-mapping-to-lipids**: Use when you have (1) peak-picked LC-MS AIF features in a feature table with m/z and retention time, (2) corresponding xcmsSet and RAMClustR pseudo-MS/MS spectral objects from centroid-mode raw data, and (3) a research goal to identify which features are lipids rather than other metabolite classes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-identifier-mapping-to-lipids) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-identifier-normalization**: Use when your metabolomics dataset contains compound identifiers in mixed formats (chemical names, InChI strings, InChIKey hashes, or SMILES notation) and you need a single canonical identifier to enable meta-analysis across multiple studies or to avoid counting the same metabolite twice under. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-identifier-normalization))
- **metabolite-identifier-synonym-merging**: Use when building an integrated pathway database from sources with incompatible ID schemes (HMDB XML, KEGG REST, Reactome ChEBI/UniProt maps, WikiPathways GPML) and the same analyte must resolve to one record despite synonym ambiguity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-synonym-merging))
- **metabolite-identifier-translation**: Use when metabolite entries from different studies report identifiers other than InChIKeys (e.g. CAS, HMDB ID, KEGG ID) and the datasets must be merged or intercompared on a common structural key. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-translation--67eb46932c891b47))
- **metabolite-identifier-translation**: Use when metabolomics data contains metabolite names or identifiers in heterogeneous database formats and a downstream method (e.g. network construction) requires KEGG or PubChem CIDs, especially for GC-TOF-MS metabolite lists including unknowns. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identifier-translation--54057b718e346ed3))
- **metabolite-identity-ground-truth-validation**: Use when after constructing candidate feature pair alignments and retention-time spline mappings in a multi-dataset LC-MS metabolomics integration workflow, when you have access to independent ground-truth annotations (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-identity-ground-truth-validation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-identity-verification-against-qc-detections**: Use when applying QC-based drift correction (lowess using pooled surrogate QCs) and you must pre-compile the list of features undetected in QCs — e.g. to confirm xanthosine 5'-monophosphate, detected only in study samples, is excluded a priori. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-identity-verification-against-qc-detections))
- **metabolite-importance-ranking**: Use when you have fine-tuned MetaboLM disease model checkpoints over 249 NMR metabolite columns and need to identify which metabolites drive predictions, especially multi-disease biomarker candidates across the 16 diseases. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-importance-ranking))
- **metabolite-in-source-fragment-annotation**: Use when processing centroid-mode AIF LC–MS chromatograms (e.g., xcms + RAMClustR outputs) and needing to assign isotopologues, adducts, and in-source fragments to a statistically pre-selected subset of features rather than the full dataset. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-in-source-fragment-annotation))
- **metabolite-intensity-alignment**: Use when you have raw lipidomic and metabolomic data files generated by the Multi-ABLE method (high-pressure liquid chromatography–mass spectrometry output) and need to prepare them for multivariate analysis to detect differential lipids and metabolites across biological samples (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-intensity-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-intensity-matrix-extraction**: Use when when you have raw mass spectrometry peak intensity data (rows = peaks with IDs, columns = individual samples) and you need to align it with metabolite annotations (peak ID → KEGG or ChEBI compound ID mappings) before performing pathway-level analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-intensity-matrix-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-intensity-trajectory-projection**: Use when joint single-cell protein and metabolite intensity tables (e.g., scSpaMet notebook 04 output for tonsil B cells) are available and the goal is to order cells along a differentiation trajectory (e.g., GC DZ to LZ) and characterize metabolite variation along pseudotime. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-intensity-trajectory-projection))
- **metabolite-interaction-network-heatmap-visualization**: Use when you have computed condition-specific Jacobian matrices from metabolomic data (e.g. via MInfer's calculate_jacobian) and need to visualize metabolite interaction patterns as a heatmap for interpretation or comparison across conditions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-interaction-network-heatmap-visualization))
- **metabolite-interaction-visualization**: Use when after computing a Jacobian matrix from covariance data in MInfer, when you need to render metabolite-to-metabolite interaction networks as publication-quality figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-interaction-visualization))
- **metabolite-ion-consolidation**: Use when after chromatographic peak detection in LC-MS data, when you have hundreds or thousands of features and need to consolidate ions presumed to originate from the same metabolite. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-ion-consolidation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-ion-species-matching**: Use when comparing untargeted LC-HRMS metabolomics experiments (e.g. T-2/HT-2 toxin detoxification products in barley) where target files show m/z and retention time shifts relative to a reference feature list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-ion-species-matching))
- **metabolite-kegg-identifier-mapping**: Use when a metabolomics feature table uses plain compound names (or HMDB/KEGG/PUBCHEM/ChEBI IDs) instead of a normalized identifier, and you need to map metabolites to HMDB IDs and KEGG pathways before running Lilikoi v2.0's pathway-based downstream analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-kegg-identifier-mapping))
- **metabolite-kegg-pathway-enrichment**: Use when you have an annotated list of metabolite compounds (with associated m/z features or compound IDs) and want to determine which KEGG metabolic pathways are significantly enriched or depleted in your experimental samples. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-kegg-pathway-enrichment) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-leakage-medium-enlargement**: Use when gap-filling multiple draft genome-scale metabolic models of a microbial community (e.g. consensus reconstructions from KBase, CarveMe, RAVEN 2.0, AuReMe/Pathway Tools) and you want the solution to respect community composition and metabolite leakage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-leakage-medium-enlargement))
- **metabolite-level-expression-summarization**: Use when after loading spatial metabolomics data (from CSV, imzML, or merged positive/negative mode files) into an AnnData object and before filtering or integrating with spatial transcriptomics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-level-expression-summarization) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-library-count-verification**: Use when a published LC-MS metabolomics workflow reports a specific reference-library size (e.g. '571 metabolites') and a library table or supplementary deposit is available whose row count must be independently verified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-library-count-verification))
- **metabolite-library-curation-ms1-rt**: Use when when you have processed authentic standards with LC-MS in positive and negative ESI modes, converted results to .mzML format, and need to build a validated in-house reference library with MS1 m/z and RT measurements for use in untargeted metabolomics compound identification workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-library-curation-ms1-rt) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-library-entry-generation**: Use when you have an experimental MS/MS spectrum (from MassBank or your own acquisition) and need to create a standardized library entry with ranked fragment ions for use in MetaboAnnotatoR or other fragment-based annotation pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-library-entry-generation) · 3 copies)
- **metabolite-likeness-fraction-analysis**: Use when you must reproduce ChemDistiller's metabolite-likeness SVM filter step or estimate what fraction of entries in compound databases (HMDB, LipidMaps, YMDB, PubChem, ZINC) is metabolite-like. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-likeness-fraction-analysis))
- **metabolite-lipid-annotation-ranking**: Use when you have LC–MS All-ion fragmentation chromatograms processed through xcms and RamClustR, a feature table with unknown identities, and you want to recover lipid annotations by matching observed spectra against lipid fragment libraries (e.g., LipidPos). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-lipid-annotation-ranking) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-list-concordance-index-calculation**: Use when you need to evaluate whether an imputation method (e.g., KNN-TN, KNN-CR, KNN-EU, zero, minimum, mean) preserves the downstream significant-metabolite list from t-tests, by comparing it against the complete-data metabolite list via the MLCI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-list-concordance-index-calculation))
- **metabolite-list-overrepresentation-enrichment**: Use when a harmonized (MMS-merged) or unmerged metabolite identifier list needs over-representation enrichment against SMPDB pathways to test whether merging changes significant pathway detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-list-overrepresentation-enrichment))
- **metabolite-list-set-intersection-analysis**: Use when you have two or more feature-selection lists (e.g. VIP &gt; 1 metabolites from PLS-DA under different data-processing strategies) and need to quantify shared vs. uniquely selected features, as in the LMM vs. M0 comparison of the CRC adipose lipid data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-list-set-intersection-analysis))
- **metabolite-lod-flagging**: Use when pre-processing targeted full-scan MSI-CE-MS data in PeakMeister and you need to distinguish true analyte peaks from near-noise signals, particularly for low-abundance serum metabolites whose signal sits near the method detection limit. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-lod-flagging))
- **metabolite-log2fc-ranking**: Use when you have metabolomics summary statistics (per-metabolite log2fc plus pval/padj columns) from a two-condition comparison and need a ranked metabolite list as input for fgsea-based metabolite set enrichment analysis (MetSEA). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-log2fc-ranking))
- **metabolite-mass-accuracy-cross-referencing**: Decision support for classifying detected LC-HRMS metabolite features as predicted vs novel by comparing observed m/z values against an in silico metabolite-prediction list within a fixed ppm tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-mass-accuracy-cross-referencing))
- **metabolite-mass-accuracy-curation**: Use when postincubation (24-h) drug metabolite features detected by LC-HRMS differential analysis need to be curated and classified against in silico predictions (e.g., BioTransformer 3.0) using a defined mass-accuracy tolerance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-mass-accuracy-curation))
- **metabolite-mass-balance-penalty-minimization**: Use when time-course GC-MS metabolomics data (relative abundance concMatrix plus timeVec) are available for a single-compartment intracellular metabolic network, and absolute concentrations or response factors must be inferred without chemical standards. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-mass-balance-penalty-minimization))
- **metabolite-mass-database-matching**: Use when after features have been grouped into empirical compounds (empCpds) with inferred molecular formulas and adduct assignments by khipu, and you need to assign putative metabolite identities at the formula level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-mass-database-matching) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-mass-filtering-by-exact-mass-tolerance**: Use when you have only an unknown metabolite's exact mass (no MS/MS spectrum) and need to prioritize which DeepMet-sampled candidate structure most likely accounts for the observed mass spectrometric signal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-mass-filtering-by-exact-mass-tolerance))
- **metabolite-mass-lookup-preparation**: Use when when beginning an untargeted LC-MS annotation workflow, before attempting to match experimental m/z peaks to metabolite identities. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-mass-lookup-preparation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-mass-shift-calculation**: Use when you need theoretical derivative m/z values for metabolites reacted with a derivatizing matrix (AMPP, FMP-10, or a custom reagent) and the reagent's mass shift per reactive site, e.g., for dopamine, GABA, spermidine panels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-mass-shift-calculation))
- **metabolite-mass-to-charge-ratio-matching**: Use when you have a negative-mode or positive-mode LC-MS feature table with observed m/z values and peak intensities, and you need to identify which metabolites (by KEGG ID) are likely represented. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-mass-to-charge-ratio-matching) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-mass-to-structure-matching**: Use when you have a table of significant m/z features plus a reference list of all detected features from LC/MS metabolomics, and you need tentative metabolite predictions (not definitive IDs) as input to pathway/network activity analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-mass-to-structure-matching))
- **metabolite-matrix-missing-value-preprocessing**: Use when a metabolomics featuredata matrix (metabolite peak intensities/concentrations, unique sample names as row names) contains missing values or zeros that must be repaired before LogTransform or any normalization step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-matrix-missing-value-preprocessing))
- **metabolite-measurement-heatmap-visualization**: Use when a MultiAssayExperiment of raw/imputed metabolite measurements needs QC inspection for outlier samples prior to normalization, especially when samples group by a trait like tumor vs normal. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-measurement-heatmap-visualization))
- **metabolite-metabolite-network-filtering-by-combined-score**: Use when a metabolomics study reports significant metabolites (e.g. absolute fold change ≥ 1, adjusted p &lt; 0.05) and you need to rank hubs or compute centrality on a chemical-chemical interaction network rather than just pathway enrichment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-metabolite-network-filtering-by-combined-score))
- **metabolite-metadata-assignment**: Use when a metabolomics count data frame (KEGG compound IDs) needs hierarchical compound-class annotation before differential analysis, volcano plots, or fold-change counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-metadata-assignment))
- **metabolite-metadata-column-matching**: Use when validating mwTab files deposited to the Metabolomics Workbench and you need to verify that metadata columns match standard naming conventions and contain values in the expected format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-metadata-column-matching) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-metadata-column-name-matching**: Use when analyzing mwTab-format metabolomics files whose METABOLITES section headers must be mapped to standardized field names and value formats for repository curation, FAIRness assessment, or quality control. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-metadata-column-name-matching))
- **metabolite-metadata-integration**: Use when when you have separate quantification data (abundance matrix), sample metadata (phenotypes, treatment groups, experimental conditions), and spectral data (MS/MS fragmentation patterns or other spectral features) that must be combined for mass spectrometry-based metabolite analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-metadata-integration) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-misidentification-simulation**: Use when assessing the robustness of metabolomics ORA to metabolite misidentification — e.g. reproducing pathway loss/gain rates at a fixed misidentification rate (4%) across datasets with KEGG-annotated differential metabolites and background lists. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-misidentification-simulation))
- **metabolite-missing-value-filtering**: Use when preprocessing urine metabolite intensity tables (e.g. synthetic_metabolomics.csv in the ACTIONdemonstrator Nextflow pipeline) to remove samples or metabolites with excessive missingness prior to normalization and scaling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-missing-value-filtering))
- **metabolite-missing-value-imputation**: Use when metabolite assay tables contain missing values (NAs) that exceed detection or instrument runtime limits, AND you have retained metabolites with ≥60% non-missing data (i.e., ≤40% missingness). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-missing-value-imputation))
- **metabolite-missing-value-mcar-mnar-classification**: Use when a metabolomics concentration table (.csv/.xlsx) contains missing metabolite measurements and values must be filled prior to normalization, transformation, and scaling, with missingness mechanism (MNAR vs MCAR) driving the choice of imputation method. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-missing-value-mcar-mnar-classification))
- **metabolite-missingness-threshold-filtering**: Use when when you have a metabolite measurement matrix with missing values across samples and need to decide which metabolites to retain before applying k-nearest neighbor imputation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-missingness-threshold-filtering))
- **metabolite-missingness-visualization**: Use when a metabolomics measurement table contains missing values and you need to inspect missingness patterns per metabolite/sample before imputation, and to decide which metabolites to exclude (missingness &gt; 40%) prior to differential analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-missingness-visualization))
- **metabolite-model-feature-ranking**: Use when you have trained one model per metabolite across multiple omics feature sets and need to identify which features are globally important or consistent regulators across the metabolome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-model-feature-ranking))
- **metabolite-module-enrichment-analysis**: Use when a metabolite co-expression/co-accumulation network (e.g. WGCNA modules built from LC-MS peak areas) must be interpreted biologically by testing whether specific ClassyFire compound classes are over-represented within each module. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-module-enrichment-analysis))
- **metabolite-molecular-property-retrieval**: Use when a metabolite namespace (e.g. MNXref) must be annotated with molecular properties for permeability classification, and identifiers need translating to PubChem CIDs before applying rule-based thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-molecular-property-retrieval))
- **metabolite-molecular-state-bookkeeping**: Use when starting CCS prediction from SMILES inputs of metabolites and you must enumerate all relevant ionization states (protonated, deprotonated, neutral) before conformation generation, ensuring agreement tables and consensus errors are attributed to the correct molecular state. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-molecular-state-bookkeeping))
- **metabolite-ms-ms-annotation**: Use when when you have SWATH-MS raw data (mzML or vendor format) containing multiplexed MS/MS spectra from multiple co-eluting precursor ions and need to separate these spectra into individual, annotatable component spectra for metabolite identification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/metabolite-ms-ms-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **metabolite-mz-matching**: Use when annotating peaks from an untargeted LC-MS metabolomics feature table (m/z per sample) with optional MS2 spectra in .mgf/.mzML format, and you need metabolite candidates from CEU Mass Mediator plus spectral-library confirmation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-mz-matching--cf6e55178fe8378d))
- **metabolite-mz-matching**: Use when you have estimated m/z values from LC-MS untargeted metabolomics data (e.g., the SMART 2.0 narcotics example) and need to assign compound identities via database m/z matching at ppm-level tolerances. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-mz-matching--b8228576fcf266ea))
- **metabolite-mz-ms2-annotation**: Use when you have ranked m/z peaks from a feature-attribution step (e.g. ensemble_RISE.npy heatmap with contribution scores) and need to translate raw spectral signals into annotated metabolites before network or pathway analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-mz-ms2-annotation))
- **metabolite-name-cross-block-matching**: Use when curating or validating a parsed mwTab-format study file and you need to confirm that metabolite names in the MS or NMR quantitative data blocks match the METABOLITES block, including columns labeled under variant names. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-name-cross-block-matching))
- **metabolite-name-standardization**: Use when a metabolomics dataset contains free-text metabolite names that must be converted to standard HMDB/SMPDB IDs before pathway mapping or pathway-based classification (e.g., Lilikoi PDS modeling). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-name-standardization))
- **metabolite-name-to-hmdb-mapping**: Use when a standalone metabolomics dataset contains free-text or non-standard compound names (e.g. urine metabolite lists like Supplementary Material S2) that must be converted to HMDB IDs before gene-set mapping and over-representation testing in metGWAS 1.0. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-name-to-hmdb-mapping))
- **metabolite-name-to-inchikey-translation**: Use when a metabolomics dataset (e.g. Metabolomics Workbench study ST001039, Supplementary Table S3) reports metabolites by non-systematic names (PubChem, ChEBI, HMDB, KEGG, LipidMaps, DrugBank, Bin ID, CAS) and InChIKeys are needed for merging. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-name-to-inchikey-translation))
- **metabolite-naming-harmonization**: Use when a metabolomics input table contains compound names or IDs with synonyms and inconsistent naming that must be resolved to standard cross-database identifiers before combining p-values and fold-changes across studies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-naming-harmonization))
- **metabolite-naming-statistics-tabulation**: Use when a merged metabolite dataset must be audited for name consistency (e.g. how many entries keep the PubChem title name, how many differ, and how many would not have merged by name alone), to justify or validate a metabolite merging strategy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/metabolite-naming-statistics-tabulation))
